1468:
1469:### Key SOTA findings
1470:
1471:1. **Fill-first selection is SOTA for 8-account 5h-window staggering** (per `selector.go:34-35` upstream comment "burns one account before moving to the next, which can help stagger rolling-window subscription caps"). Round-robin would cause SIMULTANEOUS exhaustion at hour 5 across all 8 accounts; fill-first creates STAGGERED exhaustion at hours 5/10/.../40.
1472:
1473:2. **Session-affinity maximizes prompt-cache hit rate** — extracts `metadata.user_id` matching Claude Code regex `_session_([a-f0-9-]+)$` (`selector.go:430-432`); 1h TTL matches Anthropic 1h cache extension.
1474:
1475:3. **Cache passthrough is automatic + Anthropic-canonical** — proxy auto-injects `cache_control: ephemeral` on LAST tool / LAST system / SECOND-TO-LAST user turn per Anthropic-documented hierarchy. 4-breakpoint cap + 5m/1h TTL ordering enforced.
1476:
1477:4. **OAuth uses official Claude Code ClientID** `9d1c250a-e61b-44d9-88ed-5944d1962f5e` (`anthropic_auth.go:27`) — same client that ships with Claude Code itself. Each `--claude-login` produces a separate auth file; 8 accounts = 8 JSON files in `<auth-dir>/auths/`.
1478:
1479:5. **Codex T1-T7 cross-routing via same proxy** — `OPENAI_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8317/backend-api/codex` (`server.go:367-373`). When Tier 1a codex hooks land, they auto-benefit from multi-account Codex backbone.
1480:
1481:### Throughput math (8-account + 1h cache + 50% reuse rate)
1482:
1483:- Single account: ~1500 messages / 5h
1484:- 8-account fleet (fill-first staggered): 12,000 messages / 5h
1485:- 50% prompt-cache hit rate (multi-agent intensive arc): **~24,000 messages / 5h effective** (16× single-account)
1486:- Cost reduction on cached tokens: 90% per Anthropic-documented rate (`claude_executor.go:1851`)

1813:### Wave 79 fire 2 — 3 OTHER marketplace adds BLOCKED on SSH host key + arg-shape error
1814:
1815:**Failed commands** (verbatim):
1816:```
1817:/plugin marketplace add anthropics/skills + /plugin install                                  # FAILED
1818:/plugin marketplace add anthropics/knowledge-work-plugins + /plugin install engineering@knowledge-work-plugins  # FAILED
1819:/plugin marketplace add anthropics/claude-plugins-community + /plugin install agnix@claude-plugins-community    # FAILED
1820:```
1821:
1822:**Failure modes**:
1823:1. **Argument-shape error**: `/plugin marketplace add` accepts ONE repo identifier; the literal ` + /plugin install ...` got concatenated into the argument string — would have failed even with SSH working. The `+` was intended as 2-step shorthand in the recommendation, but interpreted literally by the command parser.
1824:2. **SSH host-key verification fail**: CC's git clone via the `org/repo` shorthand defaults to `git@github.com:<org>/<repo>.git` (SSH protocol). `~/.ssh/known_hosts` is missing entirely (probed; 0 github.com entries; the file does not exist). Strict host-key checking rejects the connection.
1825:
1826:**Probed**: HTTPS reachability verified for all 3 orgs via `git ls-remote https://github.com/...` returning HEAD SHAs:
1827:- anthropics/skills: HEAD `d211d437443a7b2496a3dad9575e7dddd724c585`
1828:- anthropics/knowledge-work-plugins: HEAD `9789ea78ad66e395a9c709146cacecdc14ce2abf`
1829:- anthropics/claude-plugins-community: HEAD `f846a0bcb0e721b1f93d60e8b73e91dafc4a1e87`
1830:
1831:**SOTA recovery path** (per CR-6 fresh-from-github via OFFICIAL NATIVE CHANNEL — `git clone https://github.com/<owner>/<repo>.git` is the canonical SOTA fresh-pull mechanism):
1832:
1833:```
1834:# Step 1 — register marketplace via HTTPS URL (split as TWO commands, NOT chained with `+`)
1835:/plugin marketplace add https://github.com/anthropics/skills.git
1836:/plugin marketplace add https://github.com/anthropics/knowledge-work-plugins.git
1837:/plugin marketplace add https://github.com/anthropics/claude-plugins-community.git
1838:
1839:# Step 2 — install desired plugin(s) from each registered marketplace
1840:/plugin install <plugin-name>@skills
1841:/plugin install engineering@knowledge-work-plugins
1842:/plugin install agnix@claude-plugins-community
1843:```
1844:
1845:**Alternative** (operator preference): set up SSH known_hosts permanently:
1846:```powershell
1847:$kh = "$env:USERPROFILE\.ssh\known_hosts"
1848:if (!(Test-Path "$env:USERPROFILE\.ssh")) { mkdir "$env:USERPROFILE\.ssh" | Out-Null }
1849:ssh-keyscan -t ed25519,rsa github.com 2>$null >> $kh
1850:# Verify against published GitHub fingerprints: https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/githubs-ssh-key-fingerprints
1851:```
1852:
1853:**Recommended: HTTPS path** — no key management overhead + matches CR-6 verbatim install primitive enumeration.

2191:### Bug found & fixed
2192:
2193:**Symptom**: `POST /v1/messages → 502 unknown provider for model claude-*` despite stdout showing 7 clients.
2194:
2195:**Root cause**: my initial design assumed `auths/` subdirectory convention; but `auth-dir: "~/.cli-proxy-api"` IS the directory scanned for `.json` files directly. Files at `.cli-proxy-api/auths/*.json` were 1 level too deep. main.log confirmed: `auth directory scan complete - found 0 .json files`.
2196:

3141:
3142:### Critical lessons codified
3143:
3144:1. **RTK `--no-patch` flag is MISLEADING**: it ONLY skips settings.json patching. CLAUDE.md injection + RTK.md creation + @RTK.md reference still fire as side-effects. NEVER use `--no-patch` as dry-run.
3145:2. **RTK `-g` (global) flag writes to operator's actual `%USERPROFILE%`**, NOT redirected runtime — even when CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR / HOME env vars point elsewhere. Sibling-bleed risk EXTREME.
3146:3. **RTK project-local mode FORCES full CLAUDE.md content injection** (~140 LOC). `--hook-only` requires `--global` flag; no minimal-impact project-local path exists.
3147:4. **CR-3 Phase 1 bootstrap exception was VIOLATED** in Phase 5-7 — design-surface side-effects landed before codex T1 fired.
3148:
3149:### CR-9 install-risk lesson reinforced
3150:
3151:Pre-install REVERT check + measurement-first dry-run discipline must include reading upstream install-tool flag semantics CAREFULLY (RTK's `--no-patch` documentation was insufficient to predict actual behavior). Future install commands SHOULD test in throwaway worktree FIRST when flag semantics are uncertain.
3152:
3153:### RTK adoption decision (post-rollback)
3154:
3155:Per upstream README + dry-run findings, the install paths available are:
3156:- **Path A**: `rtk init -g --auto-patch` — global mode; sibling-bleed (CR-9 violation); auto-rewrite hook on Unix shells only
3157:- **Path B**: `rtk init` (project-local) — forces full ~140 LOC CLAUDE.md injection; no auto-rewrite hook
3158:- **Path C**: NO install — agents invoke `rtk <cmd>` explicitly when wanted (zero install footprint)
3159:
3160:For sss specifically: Path C is the SOTA-discipline choice. Path A violates CR-9. Path B inflates CLAUDE.md beyond /doctor 40K char ceiling (current ~14K + 137 LOC RTK = ~25K, OK now but constrains future expansion).
3161:
3162:**Recommendation**: DEFER RTK install indefinitely. Use rtk commands explicitly when token-savings needed (e.g., `rtk git status` instead of `git status`). Operator can choose Path A / B if/when explicit install desired.

3142-### Critical lessons codified
3143-
3144:1. **RTK `--no-patch` flag is MISLEADING**: it ONLY skips settings.json patching. CLAUDE.md injection + RTK.md creation + @RTK.md reference still fire as side-effects. NEVER use `--no-patch` as dry-run.
3145-2. **RTK `-g` (global) flag writes to operator's actual `%USERPROFILE%`**, NOT redirected runtime — even when CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR / HOME env vars point elsewhere. Sibling-bleed risk EXTREME.
3146-3. **RTK project-local mode FORCES full CLAUDE.md content injection** (~140 LOC). `--hook-only` requires `--global` flag; no minimal-impact project-local path exists.
3147-4. **CR-3 Phase 1 bootstrap exception was VIOLATED** in Phase 5-7 — design-surface side-effects landed before codex T1 fired.
3148-

3150-
3151:Pre-install REVERT check + measurement-first dry-run discipline must include reading upstream install-tool flag semantics CAREFULLY (RTK's `--no-patch` documentation was insufficient to predict actual behavior). Future install commands SHOULD test in throwaway worktree FIRST when flag semantics are uncertain.

3154-

3156:- **Path A**: `rtk init -g --auto-patch` — global mode; sibling-bleed (CR-9 violation); auto-rewrite hook on Unix shells only
3157:- **Path B**: `rtk init` (project-local) — forces full ~140 LOC CLAUDE.md injection; no auto-rewrite hook
3158:- **Path C**: NO install — agents invoke `rtk <cmd>` explicitly when wanted (zero install footprint)
3159-
3160:For sss specifically: Path C is the SOTA-discipline choice. Path A violates CR-9. Path B inflates CLAUDE.md beyond /doctor 40K char ceiling (current ~14K + 137 LOC RTK = ~25K, OK now but constrains future expansion).
3161-
